# Phase 4 retrieval evaluation

Values are means [lineage-bootstrap 95% CI]; intervals containing zero for paired differences are marked within noise. Scores use a 0–1 scale.

**DEV-selected alpha arms:** alpha=weighted-050; bm25-alpha=hybrid-bm25-weighted-025. **DEV-selected best two:** recommended, scope-explicit.
**Gold questions with a parsed version:** 101 / 212.

MRR is over the returned list (default 30); Recall is evidence-quote coverage. Models are warmed; cached latency retains the original run's measurements.

BGE guidance: [https://huggingface.co/BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5](https://huggingface.co/BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5); instruction was already on.

Skipped flagged arms: bge-base, chunks-120, chunks-440.
